2026-10-18T05:20:23.2206962Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [require-build] [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [profile] Keep the K input unmodified in the fused FP8 KV store and rerun the Firmus max bundle

Run 39097156265 (bbebad5) built the image but stopped in the pre-serving
gate after 75 s (Slurm 24489, no benchmark): check_rope_fp8_kv in
scripts/check-firmus-decode-kernels.py:266 raised "QKV input modified" on
its first non-empty case (1 row, int32 slots). The Q output and the FP8
K/V cache rows were bitwise equal to the reference; only the K input had
changed.

Cause: the token-warp QK-norm/RoPE kernel (qknorm-rope-fp8-token-warps)
kept the old fused-Q kernel's behaviour of writing the rotated BF16 K back
into the K input for every call, including the fused KV-store variant.
komoriyuta's fused KV-store kernel, which this patch rebuilds, writes the
rotated K only into the FP8 cache row and leaves the K input untouched;
his gate requires that, and his run 39094011392 (7567.56) served with
that contract, so nothing reads the BF16 K once the FP8 row is written.

Fix: in the kStoreKV instantiation skip the BF16 K writeback; the plain
variant (no fused store) still writes K in place because its caller
stores it to the cache afterwards. One fewer K store per token; the Q and
cache bytes are unchanged. Patch sha 405de87b -> 7dd3ab29. The 23-patch
SGLang chain still applies on fc275674 in build order and all five build
source checks pass on the patched tree; pytest passes.

Everything else is identical to bbebad5 (DeepGEMM series header
ff7a58cc, runtime switches, Decode cap 136, c192). The checks after
check_rope_fp8_kv (Top-K padding, fused add-RMSNorm + group quant, the
MegaMoE EP4 gate including the idle-rank 256-row case) did not run in
24489 and run for the first time here.
